【Iran foreign minister says Tehran controls Strait of Hormuz shipping, vows to act as guardian】Iran’s foreign minister wrote in the Financial Times that, by geography, Iran has unmatched control over maritime traffic in the Strait of Hormuz and that alternative routes are overhyped; the waterway is indispensable to the region and the global economy. He said Iran is determined to fulfil its historical role as guardian of the strait and of vessels with lawful passage. A solution remains possible only if the US genuinely accepts it has no military option, he added. Any further escalation in or around the Strait of Hormuz would endanger civilian lives, the welfare of all regional states and the global economy. He warned that US military action instead of diplomacy would produce greater strategic failure and said Iran will not yield to any power.

### 2026-10-21 18:45:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Crude Oil Rises to 5-Week High — Crude oil climbed to around $100 a barrel on Wednesday, its highest level since September 17 as continued attacks in the Strait of Hormuz heightened concerns over global supply security. The UK Maritime Trade Operations agency reported that a vessel had been struck near Oman, while Iran’s state-run IRIB News said a supertanker had hit a sea mine in the strait. Tehran has intensified attacks in recent weeks, with more incidents reportedly targeting vessels’ engine rooms and raising risks for maritime traffic. However, oil flows through the strategic waterway have remained relatively strong. US Secretary of State Marco Rubio said Tuesday that shipments through the chokepoint were approaching prewar levels, while data from Kpler and Vortexa showed broader Middle Eastern exports had increased since the summer. Investors are also awaiting US inventory figures due later Wednesday, after the American Petroleum Institute reported a 4.9 million-barrel decline in crude stocks last week.

【俄稱烏克蘭問題談判或有望取得進展】俄羅斯總統新聞秘書佩斯科夫今天（10月21日）表示，美國正在積極推動烏克蘭衝突的解決，當前有關談判存在取得重要進展的可能性。佩斯科夫說，美國總統特使威特科夫此次帶來了一些新的方案和建議。普京總統將認真研究美方提出的建議，俄方將根據有關情況決定下一步行動。佩斯科夫同時表示，烏克蘭問題能否實現和平解決，在很大程度上取決於烏克蘭方面的決定。他援引美國總統特朗普此前的表態稱，如果烏方一兩年前同意透過談判解決衝突，可能獲得更為有利的協議條件。佩斯科夫強調，普京對探索和平解決烏克蘭問題的道路持開放態度。俄美雙方有關接觸仍在繼續，預計今天的會談將是一次嚴肅、深入的討論，最終結果仍有待觀察。（CCTV國際時訊）

【範波會見奧樂齊中國區董事長陳佳一行】

範波代表蘇州市委、蘇州市政府對奧樂齊投資蘇州表示感謝。他說，當前蘇州正深入學習貫徹習近平總書記對江蘇、蘇州工作重要講話重要指示精神，按照市黨代會部署安排，大力實施消費投資擴容增效工程和服務業擴能提質行動，協同上海等地辦好系列促消費活動，不斷激發消費市場活力，加快建設國際化現代化人民城市。奧樂齊是全球領先的零售企業，近年來加大在蘇州全域佈局力度，體現了對蘇州營商環境和消費市場的高度認可。希望奧樂齊持續擴大在蘇投資，加密門店佈局，提高本地供應鏈配套比重，不斷拓展新業態新模式，深度融入蘇州“一刻鐘幸福生活圈”建設，更好滿足群眾多元化消費需求，讓“生活在這裡很有福氣”更加可感可及。蘇州將深入開展“三服務”專項行動，打造“有呼必應、事事閉環”營商環境品牌，全方位服務企業深耕發展。

陳佳感謝蘇州給予奧樂齊的支援幫助。她表示，蘇州經濟活力十足、市場空間廣闊、消費需求旺盛，是零售企業深耕發展的沃土。奧樂齊堅定看好蘇州發展，將堅持本土化策略、標準化模式，紮根社群拓展門店佈局，創新場景提升業態品質，持續深化與蘇州供應鏈對接合作，不斷提升蘇州區域總部能級，努力把“好品質，夠低價”的體驗帶給更多蘇州消費者，更好服務蘇州零售業創新發展。

奧樂齊中國區董事會成員、首席財務官林曉陽；副市長季晶，蘇州工業園區主要負責同志參加會見。（蘇州日報）

【Iran says it submitted seven-day plan to US to reopen Strait of Hormuz; will resume diplomacy if US shows sincerity】Iranian Foreign Minister Araghchi told the Financial Times he submitted a seven-day plan to the United States via intermediaries at last week’s UN General Assembly outlining steps to end the war and reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Slovenia Jobless Rate Steady at 4.4% — Slovenia’s unemployment rate remained unchanged at 4.4% in August 2026, matching the previous month. The number of unemployed people decreased by 26 month-on-month and 1,076 year-on-year to 43,231. Those aged 40–49 accounted for the largest share of unemployment at 24%, followed by those aged over 55 at 23.96% and those aged 30–39 at 21.6%. Meanwhile, the share of unemployed people with tertiary education rose to 19.6% from 19.3% in the previous month, while first-time job seekers accounted for 16.4%, down slightly from 16.6%. The share of those unemployed for more than a year increased to 38.4% from 37.9%, while the share of those unemployed for more than two years edged down to 20.7% from 20.9%.

### 2026-10-21 18:16:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Latvia Producer Inflation Accelerates in September — Producer prices in Latvia rose by 3.1% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 2.7% increase in the previous month. Price growth in manufacturing remained steady at 2.5%, supported by faster increases in textiles (6.3% vs. 5.4% in August), paper and paper products (4.0% vs. 2.6%), chemicals and chemical products (9.3% vs. 8.2%), and rubber and plastic products (6.5% vs. 6.2%). In contrast, price growth slowed for beverages (2.6% vs. 3.7%), wood and products of wood and cork, except furniture (4.2% vs. 5.4%), and printing and reproduction of recorded media (2.4% vs. 5.0%). Moreover, prices rose faster in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply (4.3% vs. 1.6%). Meanwhile, price growth moderated in mining and quarrying (1.9% vs. 4.9%) and water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities (8.8% vs. 9.1%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose by 0.5% in September, down from an upwardly revised 0.6% increase in August.

### 2026-10-21 18:15:33  #MKT News

【National Financial Regulatory Administration convenes IAC 2026 meeting in Beijing】China's National Financial Regulatory Administration International Advisory Committee (IAC) met in Beijing on Oct. 19-20. Director Ding Xiangqun reviewed recent progress and set regulatory priorities for the 15th Five-Year period: risk prevention, stronger supervision, promoting high-quality development and expanded high-level opening-up. Delegates discussed financial risks under the current international environment and strategies for resolving financial institution stress, and reaffirmed confidence in China's economic resilience after achievements in the 14th Five-Year period. The meeting urged stronger policy coordination and cross-border regulatory cooperation amid rising global uncertainty; enhanced cross-border risk prevention and response; guidance for financial firms to adopt AI while tightening controls on new tech-related financial risks; and continuous improvement of early-warning and resolution mechanisms to prevent systemic risk.

### 2026-10-21 18:13:12  #宏觀 #公司 #市場 #國際

【國家金融監督管理總局召開國際諮詢委員會2026年會議】國家金融監督管理總局國際諮詢委員會（IAC）2026年會議於10月19日—20日在北京召開。中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理何立峰19日晚在釣魚臺國賓館會見金融監管總局國際諮詢委員會委員時表示，中國正全面落實“十五五”規劃綱要，將繼續堅定不移擴大金融領域高水平對外開放，加快建設金融強國。歡迎更多外資金融機構來華投資興業，深化對華合作，為中國式現代化建設提供高質量金融服務。金融監管總局黨委書記、局長丁向群出席會議，介紹了中國銀行業保險業最新進展，以及“十五五”時期金融監管總局在防風險、強監管、促高質量發展、擴大高水平對外開放等方面的工作重點。會議圍繞當前國際形勢下的金融風險和監管、金融機構風險處置策略等議題進行了深入討論。與會代表充分肯定了中國在“十四五”時期取得的發展成就，表示對“十五五”時期中國經濟的韌性與長期發展前景充滿信心。會議認為，在當前國際環境不確定性上升的背景下，各國監管當局應加強政策協調和跨境監管合作，做好跨境風險防範和應對；引導金融機構在利用人工智慧等新技術提升效率的同時，加強新型金融風險防範；不斷健全金融風險預警處置機制，守住不發生系統性風險底線。美國聯邦存款保險公司前主席希拉·貝爾，香港證券及期貨事務監察委員會前主席沈聯濤，香港保險業監管局主席姚建華，安聯集團（義大利）董事長、歐洲保險業聯合會前主席塞爾吉奧·巴比諾特，友邦保險集團獨立非執行主席兼獨立非執行董事杜嘉祺，瑞穗金融集團董事長武英克，渣打集團主席羅睿思，澳門金融管理局行政管理委員會主席黃善文等委員出席了會議。金融監管總局黨委委員、副局長肖遠企、叢林，駐總局紀檢監察組組長、黨委委員楊國瑞，首席風險官、首席律師，金融監管總局相關司局和地方局負責同志，外交部、財政部、人民銀行、證監會、北京市委金融辦、西城區政府相關代表參加了會議。此次會議在2026金融街論壇年會期間舉行，國際諮詢委員會委員出席論壇年會開幕式、閉幕式和相關會議活動。

Key measures: support localities to run early pilot reforms in national high‑tech industrial development zones and national independent innovation demonstration zones; strengthen macro guidance on tech and industrial layout and push regions to build technology specializations aligned with local endowments; enhance central‑local coordination by creating shared central and local tech funding and linked implementation mechanisms for major tasks; deepen cross‑regional collaborative innovation and coordinate development of distinct science‑and‑industry functional zones. 【李強同俄羅斯總理米舒斯京共同主持中俄總理第三十一次定期會晤】10月21日上午，國務院總理李強在聖彼得堡同俄羅斯總理米舒斯京共同主持中俄總理第三十一次定期會晤。李強表示，近些年，面對複雜動盪的國際形勢，中俄雙方在兩國元首戰略引領下，攜手並肩、團結向前，以富有活力、韌性的合作對沖外部環境的不穩定不確定性，合作道路越走越寬，步伐越來越穩。當前，中俄新時代全面戰略協作夥伴關係正在進入更有作為、更快發展的新階段。中方願同俄方發揚相互尊重、互利共贏精神，推動各領域合作不斷走深走實，把兩國元首重要共識更好轉化為實際行動和具體成效，更好服務兩國發展振興，更多惠及兩國人民。米舒斯京表示，今年是《俄中睦鄰友好合作條約》簽署25週年。當前，俄中新時代全面戰略協作夥伴關係處於歷史最高水平，樹立了國家間關係的典範。面對國際形勢不確定性，兩國經貿合作勢頭良好。俄方願同中方在互利共贏、相互尊重、睦鄰友好基礎上，進一步加強各領域務實合作，維護兩國共同利益。李強和米舒斯京聽取了雙方代表彙報中俄投資合作委員會、中俄能源合作委員會、中俄人文合作委員會，中俄總理定期會晤委員會，中國東北地區和俄羅斯遠東地區政府間合作委員會工作。兩國總理充分肯定各委員會近一年來的工作。雙方一致認為，元首定向領航是中俄關系穩健發展的重要保障，要以兩國元首重要共識為根本遵循，推進經貿投資、能源資源、互聯互通、航空航天、金融、工業等重點領域合作，培育人工智慧、生物技術、數字經濟等前沿領域發展新動能，共同辦好“中俄教育年”，加強旅遊、文化、青年、媒體等人文交流，激發地方合作活力，密切多邊事務溝通協作，推動取得更多務實成果。會晤後，兩國總理簽署《中俄總理第三十一次定期會晤聯合公報》。雙方還達成經貿、航天、科技、教育等領域合作檔案。（新華社）

【China central offices urge SOEs to set up independently accounted, performance-exempt R&D reserve funds with error-tolerance】The CPC Central Committee General Office and State Council General Office issued opinions to deepen integration of technology and industrial innovation. They call for improving mechanisms for enterprises to routinely participate in national technology and industrial strategy, planning and policy-making. The National Natural Science Foundation pilot will be opened to private firms; the scope and size of corporate joint innovation funds will be expanded. The document encourages state-owned enterprises to accelerate establishment of independently accounted R&D reserve funds that are exempt from value-preservation/appreciation performance assessments and that include error-tolerance and correction provisions. It urges technology SMEs to boost R&D spending and raises the preferential R&D super-deduction rate. The guidance backs enterprise-led construction of innovation platforms, opens major national science infrastructure, large research instruments and national scientific data centers to firms, and steps up corporate support under national talent programs while strengthening enterprise postdoctoral stations.

Euro Weakens Amid French Fiscal Concerns — The euro remained under pressure around $1.122, as rising French borrowing costs and worsening tensions between the US and Europe over Russia’s war in Ukraine weighed on sentiment. France’s 10-year OAT yield climbed above 5%, its highest level since 2002, reflecting mounting concerns over France’s public finances and social unrest. Meanwhile, relations between Washington and European allies have deteriorated as US President Donald Trump deepens his engagement with Russian President Vladimir Putin. Recent moves, including easing sanctions on Moscow and questioning Ukraine’s political leadership, have heightened uncertainty over Western unity and future support for Kyiv. Elsewhere, rising oil prices amid the ongoing Iran war have intensified inflation concerns and increased expectations of tighter monetary policy, weighing on Europe’s growth outlook. Political uncertainty ahead of key elections across major European economies has added to the pressure on the single currency.

【Franklin Templeton warns markets unprepared for potential eurozone debt crisis 2.0】Franklin Templeton’s fixed-income team, led by global CIO Sonal Desai, warned sustained selling of French government bonds could escalate into a eurozone debt crisis potentially more severe than the crisis of the early 2010s and said markets are unprepared. On Tuesday the 10-year French yield premium to German Bunds topped 150bps for the first time since January 2012. Desai said France — the eurozone’s second-largest economy (GDP ~€3.0t) with roughly €3.6t of debt — would present a far larger stress test for the EU than past sovereign cases; its debt stock is more than ten times Greece’s, making simple bailout options infeasible.

### 2026-10-21 17:55:56  #MKT News #Star Stocks #NVIDIA #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Magnificent 7 #Market Regions #Europe & UK #NVDA.O #Impact bullish

【NVIDIA launches DSX POD AI factory to accelerate European infrastructure deployment】NVIDIA launched DSX POD, a factory-integrated AI factory reference design based on the Vera Rubin architecture, targeting faster European AI infrastructure rollouts. Partners build modular container units that are fully integrated and validated in manufacturing; once on site they require only connection to pre-provisioned power, cooling and networking to operate, NVIDIA said, cutting deployment time from about six months to as little as 14 days. The design moves compute, networking, cabling, rack-level power and liquid cooling installation into the factory, reducing on-site planning, construction, skilled labor and logistics and lowering risks from component delays and error-prone field work. NVIDIA said factory-tested containers shorten the path to model training and production inference, enabling customers to start generating tokens and revenue earlier. JENSEN HUANG: AI factory creates value when it starts producing tokens, not when construction finishes.

French 10-Year Yield Tops 5% — France’s 10-year OAT yield surged above 5%, reaching a new 24-year high as investors grew increasingly concerned about the country’s fragile public finances and mounting social unrest. The spread over 10-year German Bunds widened to around 155 basis points, its highest level since the eurozone sovereign debt crisis. Global bond yields have climbed this year amid surging energy prices and expectations of further monetary tightening, while highly indebted euro-area countries, including France, face additional pressure from rising borrowing costs and growing debt burdens. Meanwhile, the country's minority government faces a difficult task in passing its budget and reducing the deficit, amid parliamentary divisions, widespread protests and uncertainty ahead of the 2027 presidential election. Investors are also awaiting Moody’s long-anticipated credit rating review on Friday, with the risk of a downgrade adding to pressure on French bonds.

【分析師：印尼維持利率不變，印尼盾存在進一步走弱風險】分析師稱，在外部壓力不斷上升的背景下，印尼央行選擇維持政策利率不變、依靠激勵措施吸引資金流入，印尼盾面臨繼續貶值的風險。印尼央行將基準利率維持在 5.75%，同時下調銀行存款準備金率，以此穩定匯率、託底經濟增長。決議公佈後，印尼盾小幅走弱；雅加達綜合指數則盤中回吐最高 1.8% 的跌幅，收盤上漲 0.9%，錄得日內高點。新加坡隆巴德奧迪耶（Lombard Odier）策略師 Homin Lee 表示：“印尼央行似乎判斷，MSCI 事件給匯率市場帶來的衝擊尚且可控。但很明顯，央行希望把基準利率保留到市場需要強力政策干預時，作為最後的手段。”

【中亞人民幣融資激增 哈薩克主權財富基金連發熊貓債點心債】中亞用人民幣融資正在形成趨勢。2026年，哈薩克主權財富基金Samruk-Kazyna兩度發行人民幣債券融資，分別在4月和10月以“熊貓債”和“點心債”形式，融資各30億元。4月9日，Samruk-Kazyna在中國銀行間市場首次發行30億元、三年期熊貓債，票面利率2.18%。這筆發行，既是中亞地區發行人首次發行熊貓債，也是主權財富基金作為發行主體的首單熊貓債。 10月16日，Samruk-Kazyna在香港交易所舉行敲鐘儀式，上市該機構首批合共30億元離岸人民幣債券（即“點心債”）。發行分為兩個年期，三年期債券規模20億元，票面利率2.15%；五年期債券10億元，票面息2.3%；投資者認購需求超過發行總規模的3倍以上。債券同時在港交所和阿斯塔納國際交易所（AIX）上市交易。（財新網）

### 2026-10-21 17:29:40  #MKT News #09868.HK #Impact mixed

【He Xiaopeng says L3 safety is questionable】XPeng (09868.HK) chairman He Xiaopeng told the World Intelligent Connected Vehicles Conference that the industry should skip L3 because it shifts liability to the system without improving user experience or safety. He said L5 must be redefined—cars are passive automated platforms while robots perform active 24/7 tasks—and that prior L5 definitions focused on technology rather than safety and scene controllability. He added regulators must prioritise safety and governance for higher-level autonomy to progress, and that the commercially relevant opportunities are L2, L4 and a redefined L5, with L3 essentially an enhanced form of L2.

【何小鵬：L3是不是更安全需要打個問號】小鵬汽車董事長何小鵬今日在世界智慧網聯汽車大會上表示，自動駕駛應該跳過L3，L3模式下安全責任轉移給系統，但它只是轉移了責任，使用者體驗沒有最佳化，重點是安全性沒有變好。未來L5會重新被定義，汽車自動駕駛屬於被動式自動駕駛；而機器人是需要24小時主動執行任務的；過去L5是純從技術維度定義的，沒有從安全、場景可控角度去定義。他認為，各個國家監管體系想要管控自動駕駛、支撐技術向更高等級演進，一定要安全、可管控。基於這個邏輯判斷未來自動駕駛賽道真正有發展機會的是L2、L4和重新定義後的L5，而L3本質上只是L2的一種增強形態。（第一財經）

【惠譽：印尼經濟增速難以達到政府 8% 的目標】惠譽表示，印尼中期經濟增長預期將顯著低於政府設定的 8% 中期目標。不過這家評級機構將印尼趨勢 GDP 增速預期由 4.7% 上調至 4.9%，該水平仍低於印尼的中期增長目標。惠譽預測，印尼 2026 年 GDP 增速為 5.2%，下半年增長動能放緩。 惠譽預計，上半年集中投放財政支出後，後續財政支援力度將減弱；淨出口會對增長形成拖累。 2027 年和 2028 年，惠譽預計增速穩定在 4.9%。惠譽稱，受市場對政策可信度、財政紀律、資本市場治理的擔憂影響，今年印尼盾和對外緩衝指標承壓加劇。 惠譽同時提到，為託穩印尼盾出臺的快速應對措施，以及新央行行長的迅速任命緩解了壓力；2027 財年預算體現一定政策延續性，降低政府大幅增支的風險。

### 2026-10-21 17:15:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Iceland Jobless Rate Falls to 3-Month Low — Iceland’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate fell to 5.7% in September 2026 from a six-month high of 6.8% in the previous month. This marked the lowest reading since June, as the number of unemployed persons decreased by 2.6 thousand month-on-month to 13.3 thousand. By gender, the unemployment rate for men dropped sharply by 6.1% to 7.8 thousand, while that of women increased by 5.1% to 5.5 thousand. Moreover, the youth unemployment rate, which measures job-seekers between 16–24 years old, climbed to 16.1% from a revised 14.4% in August. Meanwhile, the employment rate rose by 75.0% from 73.8%, with the number of employed individuals rising by 2.1 thousand to 220.7 thousand. The labor force participation rate ticked up to 79.7% from 79.2%. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate fell by 5.1% in September from 6.0% in the preceding period.

Belgium Consumer Pessimism Highest in 18 Months — Belgium's consumer confidence indicator fell to -13 in October 2026, marking its eighth consecutive month in negative territory and the lowest reading since April 2025, down from -10 in September. Households' assessments of their current financial situation edged down to 18 from 19, while pessimism about major purchases deepened, with the indicator falling to -38 from -31. Saving intentions also weakened to -34 from -31. Over the next 12 months, households expect prices to continue rising, while intentions to make major purchases declined to -21 from -19. Expectations for personal financial conditions deteriorated to -7 from -2, while sentiment regarding Belgium's economic outlook worsened to -44 from -41. Meanwhile, households see unemployment rising.

Croatia Jobless Rate Steady at 3.4% — Croatia’s registered unemployment rate remained unchanged at 3.4% for the third consecutive month. The number of unemployed persons declined 1% month-on-month to 62.6 thousand, while total employment edged down 0.6% to 1.767 million. The labor force also decreased 0.6% to 1.829 million. Meanwhile, paid employment in legal entities fell 0.1% to 1.518 million, while employment in crafts, trades, and freelances dropped 3.5% to 231.8 thousand, and employment among private farmers edged down 0.2% to 17.2 thousand. Compared with September 2025, the labor market remained weaker overall, with the labor force down 1.4% and total employment declining 0.8%, while paid employment in legal entities fell 1.1%. Over the same period, the number of registered unemployed persons dropped 16%.

【聯想據悉尋求20億美元貸款 以更低成本為債務再融資】知情人士稱，聯想集團正在尋求一筆20億美元貸款，其定價將低於公司計劃再融資的一筆現有貸款。在交易稀缺之際，亞洲銀行仍熱衷於向中國大型企業放貸。因涉及非公開資訊而不願具名的知情人士稱，這筆五年期貸款已啟動銀團組建，定價為擔保隔夜融資利率(SOFR)加75個基點。這一利差明顯低於聯想2022年獲得的同等規模貸款，當時的定價為SOFR加110個基點，另加信用調整利差。知情人士表示，這一調整與基準利率從倫敦銀行間同業拆借利率(Libor)轉向SOFR有關。聯想此次籌措新貸款之際，其人工智慧基礎設施業務持續增長。截至6月的季度，公司營收躍升43%，並上調了財年業績展望。與此同時，亞洲整體貸款市場正面臨交易荒，尤其是中國借款人由於可以在境內獲得成本更低的資金，對離岸貸款的需求有限。法國巴黎銀行、花旗集團、星展銀行、滙豐控股和摩根大通香港分行為聯想這筆貸款的協調行。

【土耳其與利比亞協議或將激化能源、海域爭端】利比亞兩大政府中的第二個政府認可了這份遭到歐盟成員國希臘、塞普勒斯質疑的海上劃界協議，土耳其向擴大東地中海能源勘探又近了一步。土耳其外交部表示，利比亞東部班加西當局週二批准這份海上管轄區劃界協議，是在維護安卡拉方面的權益，同時將深化雙邊合作。利比亞西部首都的黎波里的對立政府與土耳其關係密切，此前已經支援該協議。班加西一方的認可，強化了土耳其在爭議海域的海洋主張，也可能為土耳其船隻在克里特島以東開展油氣勘探掃清道路，克里特島地處土耳其與利比亞之間。此舉還會加劇土耳其同希臘、塞普勒斯之間的緊張關係，後兩者對這片海域的大部分割槽域主張對立權利。利比亞和希臘同樣存在爭端：希臘政府已為克里特島以南海域發放海上勘探許可。

### 2026-10-21 16:50:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

Slovenia Producer Inflation at Highest Since July 2023 — Slovenia’s producer prices rose 3.9% year-on-year in September 2026, the most since July 2023, compared to 3.5% in the previous month. Prices increased for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (7.4% vs 1.9% in August), mining and quarrying (9.3% vs 8.4%), and manufacturing (3.8% vs 3.7%), with basic metals posting the steepest increase (14.3%), followed by other manufacturing (14.1%) and leather and leather products (10.2%). Meanwhile, deflation in water supply remained unchanged at 4.0%. Among the main industrial groups, prices rose for energy (6.2% vs 2.6%) and capital goods (3.3% vs 2.4%), while costs eased for intermediate goods (5.7% vs 5.8%) and were unchanged for consumer goods (1.0%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.1% in September, easing from 0.3% in August.

### 2026-10-21 16:49:15  #公司 #焦點 #國際

【範波、王維會見三星電子副社長李坽洙一行】10月20日，三星電子蘇州研發中心啟動儀式在蘇州工業園區舉行。蘇州市委書記範波會見了三星電子副社長、生產技術研究所所長李坽洙一行，並出席啟動儀式。市委副書記、市長王維參加會見。

三星電子蘇州研發中心是三星海外首家智慧製造研究所，將依託中國完備的產業鏈與人才優勢，主攻製造自動化、機器人、智慧生產系統的研發創新，支撐三星全球業務迭代升級。

範波、王維代表市委、市政府對三星電子蘇州研發中心啟動表示祝賀。範波說，蘇州是中國東部地區重要的中心城市，當前正牢牢把握上海與蘇州重點領域同城化發展、上海（長三角）國際科技創新中心關鍵支點、國家新型工業化示範區三大戰略性抓手，因地制宜發展新質生產力，著力建設“1030”產業體系，培育壯大“10+10”重點產業，打造具有國際競爭力的“智慧製造之城”。希望三星集團持續擴大在蘇產業投資和專案佈局，深化產業鏈協同，放大研發中心效應，聯合開展技術攻關、市場拓展、人才引育，實現更高水平互利共贏。蘇州將不斷擴大高水平對外開放，深入開展“三服務”專項行動，著力打造“有呼必應、事事閉環”營商環境品牌，服務企業創新發展。

李坽洙感謝蘇州給予企業的支援幫助。他說，蘇州產業基礎深厚、營商環境良好、發展前景廣闊。將以研發中心啟動為契機，匯入更多創新資源、高階人才，深化與蘇州產業鏈創新鏈對接合作，推動形成更多標誌性合作專案，共享中國市場廣闊機遇。（蘇州日報）

### 2026-10-21 16:26:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cotton Holds Steady for a Third Week — Cotton futures traded around 80 cents per pound, holding within a narrow range since early October as rising US supply and softer export demand continued to weigh on prices. The US harvest continues to advance, with 41% of the crop harvested by the week ending October 18th, above the five-year average of 37%. Meanwhile, a White House assistance program to help rural producers cover fuel costs could further support harvesting progress. Supply expectations also increased in the latest WASDE report, which raised its global production forecast for the August 2026–July 2027 season to 25.8 million tonnes from 25.66 million, reflecting higher output projections for the US and Brazil. An intensifying competition from Brazil, the world's leading cotton exporter, continues to weigh on US export prospects. Brazil's Anea projects shipments of around 3.4 million tonnes over the period, compared with WASDE's US export forecast of 2.69 million tonnes.

### 2026-10-21 16:26:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Current Account #Importance 1

Greece Current Account Surplus Widens to Highest Since 2015 — Greece’s current account surplus widened to EUR 1.743 billion in August 2026 from EUR 1.210 billion in the same month last year, marking the largest surplus since August 2015. The services account surplus increased to EUR 4.552 billion from EUR 4.498 billion, driven largely by higher revenues in travel, transport, and other services. Moreover, the primary income deficit narrowed sharply to EUR 0.021 billion from EUR 0.773 billion a year earlier. On the other hand, the goods account shortfall expanded to EUR 2.342 billion from EUR 2.199 billion, as imports (EUR 6.507 billion) outpaced exports (EUR 4.164 billion). The secondary income deficit also rose to EUR 0.447 billion from EUR 0.316 billion.

### 2026-10-21 16:19:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Georgia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

National Bank of Georgia Holds Key Rate Steady — The National Bank of Georgia held its key policy rate steady at 8.25% at its October 2026 meeting, maintaining a moderately tight stance amid persistent inflationary pressures. Annual inflation rose to 5.6% in September, well above the bank’s 3% target, with energy prices contributing around 2 percentage points. The central bank raised its 2026 inflation forecast to 5.4% from 5.2%, while expecting inflation to average 3.5% in 2027 before gradually converging toward target from the second half of the year. Economic activity remained stronger than expected, with growth averaging 7.6% in the first eight months of 2026, prompting the bank to raise its 2026 GDP growth forecast to 7.2% from 6.5%. The bank warned that further escalation in the Middle East could push energy prices and inflation higher, potentially prompting additional rate hikes, while easing tensions and lower energy prices could allow faster monetary policy normalization.

### 2026-10-21 16:17:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lebanon #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Lebanon Inflation Rate at 4-Month High — The annual inflation rate in Lebanon rose to 17.5% in September 2026 from 16.7% in the previous month, marking its highest reading since May 2026. Prices increased at a faster pace for several categories, including housing and utilities (20.4% vs. 17.9% in August), transportation (35.2% vs. 31.7%), clothing and footwear (3.1% vs. 1.2%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (9.7% vs. 9.6%), and restaurants and hotels (15.5% vs. 14.3%). Meanwhile, deflation eased for communication (-0.7% vs. -0.8%), while inflation remained unchanged for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (7.1%), education (35.7%), and miscellaneous goods and services (15.7%). In contrast, price growth slowed for food and non-alcoholic beverages (10.0% vs. 11.7%), health (7.3% vs. 7.4%), and recreation and culture (43.6% vs. 45.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.4%, unchanged from the previous month.

### 2026-10-21 16:14:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

South Africa Inflation Rises Less Than Forecast — The annual inflation rate rose for the second month to 4.5% in September 2026 from 4.4% in August, but below the expected 4.7%. Still, the headline inflation remained above the SARB’s 3% target and the upper bound of its 2%–4% tolerance band. The acceleration was largely driven by rising transport prices, which climbed further by 10.8%, compared to 8.8% in August, largely reflecting a sharper increase in fuel prices (27.9% vs 20%). Diesel prices jumped 46.0%, while petrol prices rose 25.0%, following domestic price hikes. Food inflation also rose slightly faster to 1.2% from 1.1% in August. The core inflation, which excludes prices of food, non-alcoholic beverages, fuel and energy, edged down to 4.0% in September from 4.1% in August. On a monthly basis, the CPI went up by 0.4%, after being flat in the previous month.

Indonesian Stocks Rise as BI Unveils Mixed Policy Measures — Indonesian shares rose 0.4% to 5,302 on Wednesday afternoon, reversing early losses as traders assessed Bank Indonesia’s latest policy decision. The central bank held its key rate at 5.75% for a fourth straight month but raised its deposit facility rate by 25bps to 5% and enhanced incentives for DNDF hedging to attract foreign capital. To boost liquidity, the board cut the reserve requirement ratio by 1ppt to 8% from Nov. 1, with a further easing to 7% from Dec. 1, aiming to inject around IDR 190 trillion into the financial system. Gov. Destry Damayanti said total hikes of 100bps earlier this year had helped anchor inflation expectations despite persistent El Niño-related food price pressures. Meanwhile, U.S. stock futures strengthened as concerns over AI investment eased. Healthcare, property, consumer non-cyclicals, and infrastructure drove gains. AKR Corp. jumped 6.2%, followed by Indofood Sukses Makmur (4.8%) and Indosat Tbk (4.1%). The four major banks rose between 0.3% and 1%.

UK Gilt Yields Hold Near Multi-Year Highs — The UK 10-year gilt yield remained around 5.4%, near multi-year highs, as investors assessed stronger-than-expected inflation and rising public borrowing ahead of next week’s budget and the Bank of England’s November 5 policy meeting. UK inflation rose to 3.5% in September, above the 3.4% forecast, while core and services inflation also exceeded expectations. Meanwhile, public sector net borrowing reached £24.5 billion, up from a year earlier and above forecasts. Cumulative borrowing for April to September totaled £97.8 billion, exceeding the Office for Budget Responsibility’s March projection by £6 billion. Despite the deterioration in public finances, some major investors expect the upcoming budget to emphasize fiscal discipline, potentially supporting demand for gilts. Elsewhere, resilient economic data, including a fall in unemployment and unexpected GDP growth, have reinforced expectations of tighter monetary policy, with markets pricing in a 25-basis-point rate hike in November.

### 2026-10-21 15:46:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

UK Natural Gas Prices Remain Elevated — UK natural gas prices rose above 217 pence per therm, hovering near their highest level in almost four years amid tight supply conditions. Gas exports from the Middle East, which accounted for around a fifth of global LNG supply before the war began, remained largely disrupted, with flows approximately 80% below year-earlier levels. Britain’s limited gas-storage capacity could be depleted during an exceptionally cold winter, as unusually low European gas reserves increase reliance on imported LNG. Government data show that, as of early October, the UK had storage capacity of about 3.2 billion cubic metres, leaving the country more exposed to price shocks. Meanwhile, the approval of the Jackdaw gas field could provide some relief, which is expected to start supplying gas to the UK by the end of this month and increase output by around 6% at its peak.

Polish Consumer Morale Weakest in 6 Months — Poland’s current consumer confidence indicator fell to -14.0 in October 2026 from -10.8 in September, marking its lowest reading since April as assessments of both current and future economic conditions weakened. Expectations for the country’s future economic situation recorded the sharpest decline, falling to -25.3 from -17.7, while assessments of the current economic situation dropped to -29.6 from -22.7. Households’ assessments of their current financial situation edged down to -7.3 from -7.2, while expectations for their future finances weakened to -5.2 from -4.7. Views on major purchases also deteriorated, with the indicator falling to -2.9 from -1.6. Meanwhile, the leading consumer confidence indicator declined to -10.6 in October from -7.9 in September, reflecting broad-based deterioration. Expectations for future unemployment worsened to -25.4 from -23.5, while expectations for future savings slipped to 13.5 from 14.2.

Nickel Extends Gains — Nickel traded around $15,950 per tonne, extending its rebound amid short-covering and prospects of coordinated production cuts in Indonesia. Major Chinese producers are expected to discuss output cuts at their Indonesian operations, raising hopes of tighter supply after August talks failed. However, the broader outlook remains weighed down by persistent oversupply, despite tighter Indonesian mining quotas. The International Nickel Study Group projected a 19,000-tonne global surplus in 2026, reversing its previous forecast of a 32,000-tonne deficit, with the surplus expected to widen to 70,000 tonnes in 2027. Additionally, slower-than-expected growth in battery demand, partly due to competition from lithium iron phosphate chemistries, adds to concerns that supply growth will continue to outpace consumption. The rebound's sustainability will depend on whether proposed production cuts translate into meaningful reductions in actual output.

To support the currency amid a market selloff sparked by MSCI’s warning over Indonesia’s market status, the central bank raised its deposit facility rate by 25bps to 5% and enhanced incentives for DNDF hedging to help attract foreign capital. Meanwhile, to bolster domestic liquidity, the board cut the reserve requirement ratio by 1 ppt to 8% starting Nov. 1 and further to 7% from Dec. 1, a measure expected to inject around IDR 190 trillion into the financial system. Governor Destry Damayanti noted that cumulative rate hikes of 100bps earlier this year had helped anchor inflation expectations. Globally, the dollar index steadied after touching a three-week low, helped by rising oil prices that rekindled inflation concerns and reinforced safe-haven demand for the greenback.

Turkey’s Manufacturers Turn Pessimistic in October — Turkey’s manufacturing confidence index fell further to 99.8 in October 2026 from 102 in the previous month, falling below the 100-point threshold for the first time since December 2024. Confidence in current orders declined to 85.3 from 87.8 in September, while production expectations for the next three months dropped to 107 from 115.6. Export order expectations for the next three months fell to 109.7 from 111.9, while total orders over the past three months decreased to 100.7 from 104.2. Moreover, the general business situation deteriorated further, falling to 83.9 from 90.4. Meanwhile, current finished goods stocks rose to 100.4 from 96.9, while fixed investment expenditure expectations improved to 107.2 from 104.9. Expectations for total employment over the next three months edged up to 104.5 from 104.4.

FTSE 100 Down for 3rd Session — The FTSE 100 traded lower on Wednesday, extending its decline for a third consecutive session as unexpectedly high inflation and rising government borrowing weighed on sentiment ahead of the UK budget. Annual consumer price inflation accelerated to 3.5% in September, exceeding forecasts of 3.4%, increasing pressure on the BoE to raise interest rates. Meanwhile, government borrowing reached £97.8 billion in the April–September period, £6 billion above the OBR’s March forecast. September borrowing alone totalled £24.5 billion, surpassing both projections and the year-earlier level. Banks, miners, defence and aerospace, and consumer stocks led the market lower. HSBC fell 1%, Lloyds Banking Group lost 1.2%, while Rio Tinto dropped 2.8%. Fresnillo declined more than 2% after maintaining its annual silver production outlook despite lower output year on year. Rolls-Royce and BAE Systems also fell. Utilities bucked the trend, with SSE rising 1.8% and National Grid gaining 1.6%.

### 2026-10-21 15:18:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Hits 3-Year High on on Oil Surge — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec rose to around 7.45%, reaching its highest level since March 2023, as persistently elevated crude oil prices heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations of further rate hikes. Brent crude approached $112 per barrel, its highest in five months, amid Red Sea shipping risks and threats to Saudi oil infrastructure, raising concerns over India’s import costs, the rupee and government finances. September inflation accelerated to 5.54%, with economists expecting price pressures to breach the RBI’s upper tolerance band in October. The RBI’s recent shift to a “calibrated tightening” stance has reinforced expectations of further monetary tightening, with swap markets pricing in around 100 basis points of rate hikes over the next 12 months, including a possible 50-basis-point increase in December. Investors are awaiting the release of the RBI’s October policy meeting minutes later on Wednesday for further guidance.

Pound Holds at One-Month High on Strong CPI Data — Sterling held above $1.335, its highest level in a month, as stronger-than-expected UK inflation data reinforced expectations of a Bank of England rate hike next month. UK inflation climbed to 3.5% in September, its highest level since October 2025, exceeding forecasts of 3.4%, driven by motor fuels. Core and services inflation also came in above expectations. Public sector net borrowing reached £24.5 billion, higher than forecast, ahead of next week’s budget. The data follow signs of a resilient economy, including an unexpected fall in unemployment, 0.1% GDP growth in August and a narrower trade deficit. Markets are now fully pricing in a 25-basis-point increase in Bank Rate in November. The central bank has so far been the only major central bank yet to begin a policy-tightening cycle. However, Governor Andrew Bailey’s recent remarks suggesting that a prolonged war could increase pressure on policymakers to act suggest that a rate hike may be approaching.

### 2026-10-21 15:11:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Capacity Utilization #Importance 1

Turkey Factory Capacity Usage Rises in October — Turkey’s manufacturing capacity utilization rate rose to 74.5% in October 2026 from 74.2% in the previous month, marking the highest rate recorded since June 2026. The increase was supported by higher utilization across most major industry groupings, particularly consumer goods, where the rate climbed to 72.3% from 71.4% in the previous month. Non-durable consumer goods utilization increased to 73.1% from 72.5%, while durable consumer goods rebounded to 68.5% from 66.4%. Investment goods utilization also rose to 73.4% from 73.1%, indicating improved capacity usage in industries producing capital goods. Meanwhile, intermediate goods utilization edged down to 74.4% from 74.5%, although remaining the highest among the main industry groupings.

### 2026-10-21 15:04:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Indonesia Unexpectedly Holds Rates, Hikes DFR, Cuts RRR — Bank Indonesia (BI) unexpectedly maintained its key interest rate at 5.75% for the fourth straight meeting on October 21, despite renewed pressure on the currency recently, defying expectations of a 25 bps hike. BI also kept the rate on its lending facility at 6.5% but raised the overnight deposit facility rate (DFR) by 25 bps to 5%. BI lowered banks’ reserve requirement ratio (RRR) to 7% from 9% for conventional banks and to 5.5% from 7.5% for Islamic banks. However, BI remained focused on keeping inflation within its target range while supporting GDP growth. BI raised rates by a total of 100 bps in May and June combined, bringing the policy rate to its highest level since April 2025 to attract foreign inflows and shore up the rupiah. The annual inflation rate accelerated to a three-month high of 3.28% in September, while core inflation eased to 2.84%. BI expects inflation to remain within its 2.5% ± 1% target range and has maintained its GDP growth forecast of 4.9%–5.7% for 2026.

Indonesia Loan Growth Eases to 3-Month Low — Indonesia’s loan growth rose 13.26% year-on-year in September 2026, following a 13.36% increase in August, marking the slowest growth since June. By usage, investment loans led the expansion, growing 23.2%, followed by working capital loans (11.89%) and consumer loans (4.86%). Meanwhile, undisbursed loan facilities stood at IDR 2,562.57 trillion, equivalent to 21.70% of total credit limits. Lending prospects are also supported by steady third-party fund growth of 8.22% year-on-year. The banking industry's liquid assets-to-deposits (AL/DPK) ratio stood at 24.08% in September, while the non-performing loan ratio stood at 2.11% (gross) and 0.84% (net). The central bank expects loan growth to reach 8–12% for the full year, reflecting its accommodative macroprudential stance to support the economy.

### 2026-10-21 14:12:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

UK Public Sector Borrowing Slightly Higher than Expected — UK public sector net borrowing (excluding public sector banks) totaled £24.5 billion in September 2026, slightly above £22.8 billion in the same month a year ago and market expectations of £23.9 billion. This was the second-highest September figure on record, behind only that of 2020 (not adjusted for inflation), as spending growth outpaced the rise in receipts. Total spending increased by £6.7 billion year-on-year to £123.8 billion, with central government expenditure rising by £5.9 billion to £111.3 billion, driven by spending on public services, interest, benefits and other costs. Total receipts rose by £4.9 billion to £99.3 billion, with central government revenues up by £4.5 billion to £90.1 billion on higher tax receipts. Borrowing in the financial year to September was £97.8 billion, down £4.4 billion, or 4.3%, from the previous year but £6.0 billion above the OBR’s forecast. Meanwhile, public sector net debt exceeded £3 trillion for the first time, equivalent to 94% of GDP.

### 2026-10-21 14:07:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

UK Producer Inflation Hits Near 3-1/2-Year High — Factory gate prices for UK-manufactured goods increased 4.6% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.8% rise in August and surpassing expectations of 4.3% to mark the fastest increase since April 2023. Eight of the 10 product groups made upward contributions to the annual rate, led by a jump in prices of coke and refined petroleum products (63.3% vs. 50.8% in August) amid higher energy costs. Prices for metals, fabricated metal products, and machinery rose 6.1%, unchanged from the revised figure in August. Prices of other manufactured products also increased 3.6%, matching the pace in the previous month. Meanwhile, motor vehicles and other transport equipment made the largest downward contribution, with prices falling 1.2%, followed by food products, where prices dropped 0.6%. On a monthly basis, factory gate prices climbed 0.8%, following a 0.7% rise in August and exceeding forecasts of 0.7%, marking the fastest pace in five months.

### 2026-10-21 14:05:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

UK Inflation Rate Hits 11-Month High — The UK’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.5% in September 2026, its highest since October 2025, from 3.1% in August and above expectations of 3.4%. Transport made the largest upward contribution, with inflation rising to 6.6% from 4.6%, driven mainly by motor fuels. Petrol prices rose 8.2 pence per litre month-on-month to 169.5 pence, the highest since August 2022, while diesel prices increased 10.7 pence to 192.5 pence, the highest since July 2022. Motor fuel inflation accelerated to 29.8% from 23.0%. Inflation also rose for housing and household services (5.3% vs 4.9%), communication (5.8% vs 5.3%) and recreation and culture (2.3% vs 1.6%). Food inflation was unchanged at 1.3%, while inflation for restaurants and hotels eased to 3.8% from 4.1%. Core CPI inflation increased to 2.8% from 2.6%, with goods inflation rising to 3.2% from 2.7% and services inflation to 3.7% from 3.4%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.4%, following a 0.5% increase in August.

【阿迪達斯2026年第三季度營收同比增長7%，上調全年業績指引】10月21日，阿迪達斯全球公佈了2026年第三季度初步業績：全球營收達72.52億歐元，在貨幣中性下（下同），同比增長7%；營業利潤達10.08億歐元；毛利率提升2.5個百分點至54.3%。第三季度，阿迪達斯全球收到了美國關稅的大部分退款，並據此確認了約2億歐元的退稅，這部分退稅對毛利率的改善做出了貢獻。阿迪達斯全球CEO古爾登表示，“對阿迪達斯而言，這是一個非常出色的季度。我們實現了有史以來最高的單季營收——72.52億歐元，以及有史以來最高的單季營業利潤——10.08億歐元。我們的品牌、產品和人才，是我們最大的力量所在。”介面新聞瞭解到，基於第三季度超預期的表現，阿迪達斯全球上調了2026年全年業績指引，公司預計將實現9%-10%的營收增長，營業利潤預計將提升至25億歐元（此前預計為23億歐元）。

【特朗普考慮改建白宮新聞簡報室 擬恢復為羅斯福時期室內游泳池】據紐約時報報道，8 月下旬，卡洛琳・萊維特在橢圓形辦公室和特朗普的最後幾場會談中，特朗普當著多名高階幕僚向她提出一項構想：把記者遷出詹姆斯・S・布雷迪新聞簡報室，實質上就是把新聞機構移出白宮。他告訴萊維特，這件事會成為她的 “最後一項遺產”。三位知情、但未獲公開發言授權的人士表示，在萊維特任職期間，該方案始終沒有推進。過去數月，總統已經多次向她提出這項要求。萊維特已於 8 月 27 日正式卸任白宮新聞秘書。特朗普向幕僚表示，他希望恢復羅斯福 1933 年修建的室內泳池，當年修建泳池是為了給罹患小兒麻痺症、腿部行動不便的羅斯福做康復；後來尼克松把泳池加蓋封閉，騰出空間給媒體使用。持有白宮採訪資質的記者，就在簡報室相連的一間小型辦公室內辦公。相較於特朗普長期對新聞記者的抨擊，將媒體遷出白宮這件事本身議題規模不算很大，但具備強烈象徵意義。媒體緊鄰美國行政權力核心，長期以來被視作憲政民主體制中新聞自由重要性的體現。

NZX 50 Ends 1% Higher Ahead of Inflation Data — The NZX 50 advanced 130 points, or 1.0%, to close at 13,761 on Wednesday, erasing losses from the previous three sessions and moving away from its lowest level since September 16, touched a day earlier, ahead of the release of inflation data. Q3 inflation data will be released on Thursday, with the market expecting inflation to ease on both a yearly and quarterly basis. Easing Treasury yields and moderating oil prices also supported sentiment, easing inflation concerns and fears of interest rate hikes after the RBNZ raised interest rates for the second straight time in July and September. The broader index tracked gains in US stock futures as traders assessed earnings reports. Materials, financials, communication services, healthcare, and industrial stocks mainly drove the index, with notable gains from Ebos Group (5.5%), Vulcan Steel (5.2%), Mainfreight (3.2%), Fletcher Building (3.1%), Westpac Banking Corp. Thailand Stocks Ease to 3-Week Low — Thailand’s SET50 Index extended losses for a third session by noon Wednesday, slipping toward 1,038 and touching its lowest level since early October. Sentiment weakened after September consumer confidence fell from a six-month high, while headline inflation hit a five-month peak of 2.82%. Still, gains in U.S. stock futures helped limit the downside as markets assessed the latest corporate earnings releases. Locally, Bangkok last week approved a THB 50 billion housing loan program aimed at helping low- and middle-income households access homeownership. The scheme is also designed to support economic activity, with authorities estimating it could generate up to THB 125 billion in property-related output. Sector-wise, industrial services, energy, and transport stocks led the decline, outweighing gains in communications, consumer durables, and financials. Among the laggards, Delta Electronics fell 4.1%, followed by Kiatnakin Phatra Bank (-2.6%), Banpu (-1.4%), and Bangkok Bank (-1.1%).

Sensex Rebounds on IT Gains — India’s BSE Sensex rose about 0.8% to 74,401 on Wednesday, rebounding from the previous session’s losses as optimism over IT earnings lifted technology stocks, while investors tracked quarterly results and the Jio Platforms IPO. The $3.2 billion IPO of Reliance Industries’ digital subsidiary opened for subscription on Wednesday, testing appetite for large public offerings. Among stocks in focus, Bajaj Finance reported a 28.7% year-on-year increase in quarterly profit, while Hindustan Zinc’s profit more than doubled on higher metal prices. L&T Technology Services also posted higher revenue and profit, with an upbeat outlook for the second half of fiscal 2027. Meanwhile, elevated oil prices remained a risk to sentiment, with Brent crude rising to around $110 a barrel amid escalating Middle East supply concerns and a drawdown in US crude inventories. Notable gainers among technology stocks included TCS (2.7%), Infosys (3.4%), HCL (1.9%), and Tech Mahindra (1.7%).

### 2026-10-21 12:47:28  #宏觀 #國際

【中方回應COP31電氣化倡議：應充分考慮各國國情差異尋求最大公約數】10月21日上午，國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會，生態環境部副部長李高介紹中國積極應對氣候變化、參與引領全球氣候治理有關情況，並答記者問。

《聯合國氣候變化框架公約》第三十一次締約方大會（COP31）將於11月9日至20日在土耳其安塔利亞舉行，李高將再次擔任中國代表團團長。中方支援COP31聚焦“落實”，推動多邊程序為各方特別是發展中國家落實國家自主貢獻提供支援、創造條件。

有外媒提問，中方是否支援締約方大會主席關於在安塔利亞確立到2035年實現35%電氣化目標的提議？中方是否支援一個專門聚焦綠色電力的版本？

李高表示，COP31的主席國土耳其發起了電氣化倡議，提出到2035年全球電氣化率35%的目標，由參與的國家自主決定相關的政策和行動。中方認為，提高電氣化水平有助於促進能源綠色低碳轉型和應對氣候變化，中國也正在積極推進新型能源體系和新型電力系統建設，提高終端用能電氣化的水平也是我們的政策方向。

“我們讚賞主席國為推動多邊程序所做的積極努力。我們注意到，倡議得到了不少國家的支援，同時也存在一些不同的意見。有的認為應該聚焦落實已經確立的目標，有的認為要強化綠色電氣化的目標，還有的認為，作為一個自願倡議，不需要搞這種進展的評估等。我們建議主席國進一步充分聽取各方面的意見，充分考慮各國的國情差異，尋求最大公約數，爭取最廣泛支援。”李高說。

Revenue rose 59% YoY and 12% QoQ to 165 bln yuan, broadly tracking battery shipments. Gross margin fell 5.1pp YoY and 2.5pp QoQ to 20.7%, driven by a mix shift toward higher domestic energy-storage sales. Net profit was 21.2 bln yuan, up 15% YoY but down 6% QoQ; net margin declined 4.9pp YoY and 2.4pp QoQ to 12.9%. Management attributed margin pressure to higher battery consumption taxes, reduced export VAT rebates and increased asset-impairment losses. Nomura expects global battery demand to remain resilient over the long term—supported by rising EV penetration in Europe and emerging markets and by energy-security and AI/data-center deployments driving storage demand—and sees planned global capacity expansion progressing on schedule.

### 2026-10-21 12:19:11  #宏觀 #國際

【2026年亞太經合組織財長會在香港舉行】 2026年10月20日至21日，第33屆亞太經合組織（APEC）財長會在中國香港特別行政區舉行。財政部部長藍佛安作為會議主席主持會議並致辭，副部長廖岷代表中方發言。2026年APEC高官會主席陳旭出席會議併發言。香港特別行政區行政長官李家超出席歡迎晚宴。來自APEC成員經濟體、APEC觀察員以及有關國際組織等共300餘名代表參會。 

會議聚焦APEC中國年“建設亞太共同體、促進共同繁榮”主題，圍繞全球和區域宏觀經濟金融形勢、支援民生髮展的財政政策、數字基礎設施融資、因地制宜促進普惠金融發展和促進APEC青年財經人才交流合作等議題進行了討論。會議審議透過《2026年APEC財長聯合宣告》和《亞太地區數字基礎設施融資政策宣告》，為亞太經濟體深化財金合作指明瞭方向。會議還形成《支援民生髮展與投資於人——推動亞太地區可持續增長的財政政策報告》《亞太地區普惠金融創新實踐案例集》等成果檔案，並同意繼續推進亞太地區青年財經人才交流與合作。 

藍佛安指出，當前國際形勢變亂交織，地緣衝突影響外溢，世界經濟增長面臨多重挑戰。APEC作為亞太地區主要經濟合作平臺，積極促進成員開放合作，加強宏觀政策協調，推動區域經濟一體化、助力亞太地區共同發展繁榮。各方應堅定落實APEC領導人非正式會議共識，堅持多邊主義，深化財金領域務實合作，為區域經濟金融穩定和長期可持續增長作出貢獻。 

藍佛安介紹了中國經濟持續向新向優向好的態勢，強調中國將堅定不移推動高質量發展，加快培育增長新動能，著力擴大國內需求，持續擴大高水平對外開放，以中國式現代化新成就，為亞太和世界發展提供新機遇。 

會前還舉行了財長閉門會、財長與亞太工商界代表工作午餐會，分別圍繞在變動的世界中推動亞太經濟增長、動員私人資本支援數字基礎設施發展等主題開展了討論，取得良好效果。 

會議期間，藍佛安應約分別與汶萊財政部主管部長劉光明、越南財政部部長吳文俊、澳大利亞國庫部部長吉姆·查默斯、新加坡交通部部長兼財政部第二部長蕭振祥等舉行雙邊會見，就全球及區域宏觀經濟形勢，中國同相關經濟體在APEC、東盟與中日韓框架下合作以及雙邊合作等議題交換意見。藍佛安還會見了香港特別行政區行政長官李家超。

Palm Oil Nears MYR 4,800 as Profit-Taking Offsets Demand — Malaysian palm oil futures edged lower towards MYR 4,800 per tonne as traders took profits after prices approached a four-week high. Sentiment was also dampened by record inventories of 3.51 million tonnes in September, while seasonal production is expected to stay elevated in October after six consecutive monthly rises. Still, losses were tempered by firmer edible oil prices on the Dalian and Chicago markets. Exports offered support, with cargo surveyors reporting a 43.2%–58.3% mom surge in palm oil shipments during the first half of October. Festival-related demand from India, a key importer, further lifted risk appetite. Meanwhile, Indonesia raised its October crude palm oil reference price to USD 1,042.15 per tonne, while rising crude oil prices enhanced palm oil’s competitiveness as a biodiesel feedstock. Traders now monitor Malaysia’s consideration of a waiver or reduction in its crude palm oil export tax, which could improve its competitiveness against Indonesian supplies.

【Former BOJ executive expects rate hikes roughly every three months】Former BOJ executive Eiji Maeda said the Bank of Japan may continue raising rates roughly every three months to prevent inflation from deviating from its price-stability target. He expects the next hike in December and a further move possibly in March or April, saying the current pace—about one hike every three months—is appropriate and broadly consistent with market expectations. Maeda did not rule out consecutive hikes at successive policy meetings if the yen plunged, but judged that scenario unlikely now and warned back-to-back increases would boost market expectations of further tightening and pose significant risks, giving the BOJ reason to avoid consecutive-meeting hikes.

### 2026-10-21 12:02:49  #央行 #國際

【日本央行前理事前田榮治預計央行料維持每三個月加息一次的步伐】日本央行前理事前田榮治表示，為防範通脹偏離目標的風險，日本央行可能繼續保持大約每三個月加息一次的節奏。“我預計下一次加息會在12月，之後是明年3月或4月，”前田榮治週二接受採訪時表示，“目前加息步伐已經加快至大約每三個月一次，我認為這是合適的。”他曾負責日本央行新冠疫情初期的政策應對。這一判斷大體符合市場定價。隔夜掉期市場顯示日本央行不晚於12月加息的機率約為80%。市場普遍預計，日本央行將在10月30日舉行的下一次政策會議上維持利率不變。前田表示，不完全排除日本央行未來在連續召開的貨幣政策會議上接連加息的可能性，例如日元大幅貶值時就可能出現這種情況。但他認為目前看來這種可能性不大。他說，如果日本央行連續加息，市場可能會愈發預期下一次會議繼續行動，這將帶來重大風險，因此日本央行有理由避免連續會議加息。

Gasoline Futures Rebound — US gasoline futures rose to around $3.15 per gallon after hitting a seven-week low in the previous session, as persistent supply risks offset prospects for improved fuel availability. Unconfirmed reports of a proposal to revive talks between Iran and the US had pressured prices, while the US Energy Department said it was working with domestic refiners to explore ways to boost fuel output and ease prices. Meanwhile, President Trump's decision to waive key sanctions and tariffs on Russia raised prospects for increased Russian fuel exports. However, Morgan Stanley warned that oil-product markets could remain tight through mid-2027 as planned refinery outages rise sharply, potentially constraining supplies even if war-related disruptions ease. EIA data showed that US gasoline inventories rose by 3.5 million barrels in the week ended October 9, marking a second consecutive weekly build and signaling a recovery in domestic supply.

【Democratic groups sue Trump administration, DOJ over alleged interference in state election administration】National Democratic organizations sued President Trump and the U.S. Department of Justice on Tuesday, alleging the administration is coordinating actions intended to undermine public confidence in the upcoming U.S. midterms by pressuring states on ballot handling. The complaint says the government has asked California, Nevada and other states to pause processing ballots suspected of involving non-citizens and to set aside tens of thousands of mailed ballots until citizenship can be verified. Filed in Washington by the Democratic Senatorial Campaign Committee and Democratic Congressional Campaign Committee, the suit argues the federal data the requests rely on is "low-quality" and often erroneous. 【天津出臺公積金新規新增提取情形】天津市住房公積金管理中心21日對外發布相關通知，新增自住住房物業費、新購自住住房裝修費和自住住房裝修翻新費提取情形，將住房公積金使用範圍進一步拓展至住房持有期間的相關支出。 根據新政策，在天津市行政區域內持有住房的，職工及其配偶每年12月31日（含當日）前可提取一次住房公積金支付物業費，每年提取金額合計不超過3000元。 對於新購自住住房，在天津市行政區域內購買住房，符合本市購房提取住房公積金條件的，職工及其配偶可在新建住房購房合同確定的交付之日起兩年內、二手住房取得住房產權之日起兩年內就該套住房提取一次住房公積金支付住房裝修費用，提取金額合計不超過每平方米1500元乘以所購住房建築面積，且不超過15萬元。 此外，對於裝修翻新需求，在天津市行政區域內持有同一套住房滿10年且近一年內未提取住房公積金用於購房款、首付款或償還住房貸款本息的，職工及其配偶可就該套住房提取一次住房公積金用於廚衛、門窗、牆面等區域性修繕改造，改善居住條件，提取金額合計不超過3萬元。 為保障職工權益，通知還明確了相關提取業務的辦理期限調整安排。其中，規定新購住房裝修費提取截止日期在2026年9月20日至2026年12月31日期間的，提取截止日期順延6個月；2026年物業費未在2026年12月31日前提取的，提取截止日期順延至2027年6月30日。 通知自2026年10月21日起施行。

### 2026-10-21 11:28:46  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Morgan Stanley warns global refined-fuel supply could remain tight through mid-2027】Morgan Stanley says even if current war-related supply disruptions ease, global refined-product markets could remain tight through mid-2027 as unusually large refinery maintenance is constraining supply. Analysts led by Martin Latz expect planned refinery outages through Q2 next year to remove about 3.4 mln b/d of capacity — roughly 2.5 times normal and larger than current reductions from Middle East and Russian refiners. Eight months of Middle East conflict and recent reciprocal attacks on energy infrastructure between Russia and Ukraine have exacerbated the shortfall. Diesel exports have dropped sharply, pushing diesel prices above crude; diesel is critical for transport, heating and industry. Morgan Stanley says many refineries delayed maintenance to chase margins but cannot defer it indefinitely, and run rates are unlikely to rebound materially by Q2 next year.

Rupiah Holds Gains as BI Rate Decision Looms — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,970 per dollar on Wednesday, extending strength for a third session as the US dollar remained under pressure after the dollar index fell to a three-week low. Softer US Treasury yields and easing crude oil prices reduced support for the greenback, helping lift emerging market currencies, including rupiah. Traders awaited Bank Indonesia's policy decision later today, with markets widely expecting the central bank to hold its key rate at 5.75% for a fourth straight month following 100bps of cumulative tightening earlier this year. Sentiment was also aided by receding concerns over MSCI potentially reclassifying Indonesia as a standalone market, after regulators launched measures to address concentrated share ownership and strengthen shareholder disclosure requirements. However, the upside was capped by persistent fiscal concerns and renewed cost pressures, as El Niño-related food prices accelerated and September inflation hit a three-month high.

Yen Holds Steady Amid Sluggish Export Growth — The Japanese yen was little changed around 158.2 per dollar on Wednesday, remaining within a narrow range as investors assessed data showing that exports increased less than expected in September and posted the slowest growth since April. Still, exports extended their growth streak to 13 consecutive months, supported by strong demand for semiconductors and AI-related products. On the monetary policy front, the Bank of Japan is widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged next week before implementing a rate hike in December. Earlier this week, Governor Kazuo Ueda noted that underlying inflation was nearing the central bank’s 2% target, reinforcing expectations of a more hawkish policy stance. However, the yen remained under pressure from persistent carry trades, as Japan’s relatively low interest rates continued to weigh on its appeal. The BOJ’s gradual approach to monetary tightening is also viewed as insufficient to close the interest rate gap with other major economies.

US Natgas Prices Edge Higher — US natural gas prices edged up to $2.97 per MMBtu on Wednesday, attempting to rebound from a four-week low hit in the previous session, as improving LNG export activity and reduced output partly offset weak demand due to mild weather. Average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export terminals rose to 18.2 bcfd so far in October from 17.9 bcfd in September. Meanwhile, gas production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 bcfd this month, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in August and September. The storage surplus also continued to shrink, with inventories as of October 9 standing 3.9% below year-earlier levels and just 1.3% above the five-year average. Meanwhile, mild temperatures are expected to persist through late October, curbing both heating and cooling demand, while expectations of a “Super El Niño” further weigh on the winter demand outlook.

【瑞銀：降寧德時代目標價至730港元，單位利潤因產品結構及退稅變動而承壓】瑞銀髮表研報指，寧德時代第三季淨利潤為212億元，同比增長15%，但環比下跌6%，較買方預期低約10%。首九個月淨利潤為645億元，僅達全年市場共識與該行預估的62%至67%，這可能導致市場對全年共識預期的下修。若排除非經常性專案，第三季淨利潤為189億元，同比增15%，但環比降10%。期內電池銷量超過260GWh，當中儲能系統(ESS)佔約25%，電動車電池佔約75%。該行指出，按此計算的單位利潤降至每千瓦時82元，創歷史新低，主要受季度產品組合波動、低增值專案增加、9月1日起對鋰離子電池徵收消費稅，以及出口增值稅退稅率由9%降至6%影響。不過，管理層對2027年行業20%至30%的增長前景仍保持信心。瑞銀相應將該公司2026至2028年淨利潤預測下調11%至14%，降至介乎900億至1310億元，目標價由820港元下調至730港元，維持“買入”評級。

New Zealand Credit Card Spending Rises 6.9% in September — Seasonally adjusted total billings in New Zealand rose 6.9% year-on-year to NZD 4.7 billion in September 2026, following an upwardly revised 3.6% increase in August. It was the seventh consecutive month of growth, driven by higher domestic billings on cards issued in New Zealand, which climbed 5.3% to NZD 4.0 billion, while overseas billings grew 3.9%. Meanwhile, total credit limits edged down to NZD 20.8 billion, while credit limit utilisation increased to 28.7% at the end of September. The weighted average effective interest rate on personal interest-bearing advances remained at 20.0% in August 2026.

【證監會商慶軍：促進北交所新三板一體化高質量發展 三方面持續發力】中國證監會公眾公司監管司一級巡視員商慶軍10月21日在2026金融街論壇年會“共建服務中小企業資本市場新生態”平行論壇上表示，證監會將深入貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，堅持穩中求進總基調，以防風險、強監管、促發展為主線，持續健全監管制度體系，促進北交所與新三板一體化高質量發展，更好服務“十五五”規劃落地和金融強國建設。商慶軍介紹，下一步將重點從三方面推進工作：一是厚植優質企業創新發展基礎。立足服務創新型中小企業定位，釋出北交所創新性評價標準，精準識別各類創新要素，明確申報預期，為處於行業前沿、具有創新能力、持續增長潛力和專業化發展優勢的創新型中小企業提供更好服務，持續最佳化上市公司結構。同時，強化新三板規範培育功能，健全准入和退出機制，最佳化分層管理，增強創新層品牌效應和融資配置功能，完善差異化資訊披露安排，鞏固北交所與新三板“層層遞進、上下聯動、頭部引領、底層助推”的發展格局，為專精特新企業提供持續接力、精準識別的資本市場服務，更好促進新質生產力發展。二是最佳化投資生態，增強市場內在韌性。長期資金是市場“壓艙石”和“穩定器”。下一步將加大投資端建設力度，統籌促進一二級市場平衡發展，健全投資和融資相協調的資本市場功能。一方面，完善北交所發行承銷制度，穩妥實施新股市值配售，提升二級市場投資者獲得感，促進新股合理定價，持續推動詢價發行案例落地，進一步提升定價彈性；另一方面，加快建設市場產品體系，加大公募基金供給力度，穩步推出北證50ETF，促進投融資兩端平衡發展。同時，完善風險監測預警和應急處置機制，切實維護市場秩序，保護投資者合法權益。三是堅持嚴監嚴管，提升上市公司質量。依託上市公司治理專項行動，督促上市中小企業提升公司治理水平；支援獨立董事、審計委員會主動、有效履職，調動機構投資者和中小股東參與公司治理積極性，強化公司內部監督與制衡。加強對大股東、實際控制人的規範約束，緊盯上市公司“關鍵少數”，對欺詐發行、財務造假、資金佔用等嚴重侵害投資者合法權益的行為及時採取行動，嚴厲打擊重大違法行為，堅決肅清違法違規亂象。（上證報）

【新方法可引導合成分子形成特定三維結構】芬蘭研究人員開發出一種新方法，可透過在人工合成分子中編碼不同層級的資訊，引導其自組裝形成特定的三維結構。這一成果有望為開發智慧材料和分子尺度器件等提供新思路。 在生物體中，蛋白質等分子能夠根據其分子序列中編碼的資訊，自發形成特定的三維結構。然而如何讓人工合成分子也能如此精確地形成預定結構，一直是化學研究面臨的一大挑戰。 芬蘭于韋斯屈萊大學研究人員開發出一種分級的序列控制方法，透過在分子中編碼兩級資訊，分步控制其自組裝過程。第一級資訊進行初步選擇，將分子的可能結構限定為4種型別的螺旋。第二級資訊進一步引導在這4種螺旋中形成一種特定螺旋：逆時針三螺旋，即三條分子鏈相互纏繞、逆時針盤旋。與此同時，這種分級控制還會有效抑制其他可能結構的形成。 研究表明，透過對分子序列進行精細調整，可以精確控制分子最終形成的三維結構。研究團隊希望進一步提高對分子自組裝過程的預測和調控能力，使人工合成分子能夠按照預先設計的方式，形成更加複雜的三維結構。 相關研究成果已發表在學術期刊《應用化學國際版》上。(新華社)

Indonesian Shares Retreat Ahead of BI Rate Call — Indonesian equities fell 84 points, or 1.6%, to 5,197 on Wednesday morning, reversing a prior rally after regulatory measures aimed at regaining investor confidence, including adjustments to stocks with high shareholding concentration and greater disclosure of shareholder affiliations. Traders turned cautious ahead of Bank Indonesia's rate decision later today. While the central bank is expected to hold borrowing costs for a fourth month, room for easing is limited after a cumulative 100bps hike in May and June. Still, the decline was capped by record FDI in Q3 2026, lifted by solid inflows into the base metal industry. In top trading partner China, key lending rates were left unchanged for a 17th month after stronger-than-expected Q3 GDP. Cyclicals, basic materials, and financials were mainly lower, partly offset by strength in infrastructure, non-cyclicals, and transport. Notable decliners included Solusi Energi Digital (-6.9%), Mitra Adiperkasa (-6.0%), and Medco Energi (-3.9%).

【我國加強頂層設計 努力降低氣候變化風險和損害】在國務院新聞辦公室今天（21日）上午舉行的新聞釋出會上，生態環境部副部長李高介紹，生態環境部正在會同有關部門進一步加強頂層設計，努力降低氣候變化風險和損害。近年來氣候變化影響持續加劇，極端災害造成重大人員傷亡和財產損失。氣候變化正在從未來的風險隱患演變為現實緊迫的危機。我國是氣候變化敏感區和影響顯著區，氣候變化背景下各種極端災害事件對我國糧食生產、生態安全、基礎設施、城市執行和人民生命健康等造成嚴重威脅。李高介紹，我國堅持減緩和適應並重原則，加強氣候變化監測預警和風險管理，提升重點領域適應氣候變化能力，推動將適應氣候變化納入經濟社會發展的各領域各方面，提高脆弱人群的風險防範和健康適應能力。全國31個省（區、市）已印發適應行動方案，公共衛生與健康領域釋出《國家氣候變化健康適應行動方案》。生態環境部已指導39個城市深化氣候適應型城市建設試點，形成了不少好經驗好做法，比如將適應氣候變化融入城市規劃佈局、開展定性定量相結合的氣候變化風險評估、利用人工智慧提升預報預警能力等。深圳、海口、成都等試點城市積極探索建設氣候適應型社群，取得良好效果。

Taiwan Stocks Rebound from Near 5-Week Low — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 860 points, or 1.9%, to 47,153 in Wednesday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous two sessions and tracking gains on Wall Street overnight amid easing Treasury yields and oil prices. The broader index moved away from its lowest level since September 17, touched a day earlier, mainly boosted by gains in electronic technology and finance. Electronic technology rebounded, rising 1.5% after falling 1.0% in the previous session due to concerns over AI investment. Moderating oil prices lifted sentiment, easing concerns about inflation risks and interest rate hikes. TSMC, the world’s largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, recovered, rising 1.0%. 【黃河干流寧夏段連續9年保持Ⅱ類水質】記者20日從寧夏生態環境廳獲悉，“十四五”以來，寧夏不斷加強水生態環境保護與修復，全區水生態環境質量持續穩定向好。截至2025年底，黃河干流寧夏段已連續9年穩定保持Ⅱ類水質，地表水國控斷面Ⅲ類及以上優良水質比例達到85%，國家監測的15個城市集中式飲用水水源地水質全部達標並保持優良。 近年來，寧夏強化重點斷面水質管控，實時監測水質變化，採取針對性防控措施，確保水質穩定達標。持續開展排汙口排查整治，累計排查各類排汙口4682個，逐項整改銷號，整治率達到97.1%。透過實施重點入黃排水溝生態治理工程，全區22條重點入黃排水溝水質全部達到或優於地表水Ⅳ類標準。全面推行河湖長制以來，寧夏累計規範整治236處黃河寧夏段“亂佔、亂建”問題。 另外，寧夏推進汙水處理提質增效，切實鞏固黑臭水體治理成效。目前，全區已建成城市生活汙水處理廠全部達到一級A排放標準，城市生活汙水處理率、收集率分別達到99.15%、79.8%。全區地級城市建成區13條黑臭水體已全部消除，未出現返黑返臭；12個縣城建成區排查未發現黑臭水體。(新華社)

【壁仞科技魏明：算力閒置的另一面，是高階算力“非常缺”】2026可持續全球領導者大會於10月20日-22日在上海黃浦世博園區舉辦。在《以東方力量推進高質量發展》行業主題論壇上，壁仞科技戰略規劃高階副總裁魏明指出，算力市場呈現明顯的結構性矛盾：一方面部分算力處於閒置狀態，另一方面高階算力卻“非常缺”。“如果問大模型公司今年最挑戰的事情是什麼，那就是缺算力，缺高階算力。”魏明表示。他介紹，目前國產高階晶片的算力已經起來，能夠提供相應服務，這為緩解高階算力緊缺提供了現實支撐。與此同時，閒置算力也可透過PD分離等方式盤活——把算力較強的國產卡與其他視訊記憶體較強卡配合使用，讓不同晶片各展所長，以新技術逐步化解閒置問題。在魏明看來，隨著國產晶片能力提升與排程技術不斷成熟，閒置與緊缺並存的矛盾有望逐步緩解，他對國產卡的應用前景充滿信心。

### 2026-10-21 10:10:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Natural Gas Rebounds — European natural gas prices rose to €87/MWh on Wednesday, moving back near a multi-year high after sliding more than 3% in the previous session as traders weighed mixed reports on efforts to end the Middle East war. Tuesday’s decline came after unconfirmed reports that Qatar and Pakistan had submitted a new proposal aimed at reviving talks to end the conflict. However, a diplomat involved in the negotiations said reports of an imminent agreement were incorrect. European gas prices remain more than double their pre-conflict levels amid ongoing disruptions to LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and unusually low inventories. As of October 20, European storage facilities were 74.62% full, the lowest seasonal level since records began in 2009. The situation is particularly concerning in Germany, Europe’s largest economy, with storage levels just above 61%. Strong LNG demand from Asia also increases the risk of more intense competition for cargoes when colder weather arrives.

【黔西南州城市建設投資集團原總經理潘維維被“雙開”】經貴州省委批准，貴州省紀委監委對黔西南州城市建設投資（集團）有限公司原黨委副書記、總經理潘維維嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，潘維維喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，政績觀扭曲錯位，背離高質量發展要求；違反中央八項規定精神，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；背離組織原則，個人或少數人決定重大問題；不正確履行職責，造成不良影響；目無法紀，挪用公款歸個人使用；利用職務便利為他人謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。潘維維嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌挪用公款、受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經貴州省紀委常委會會議研究並報貴州省委批准，決定給予潘維維開除黨籍處分；由貴州省監委給予其開除公職處分，終止其中國共產黨黔西南州第八次代表大會代表資格，將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

Hong Kong Stocks Rebound on AI Optimism and Bargain Hunting — The Hang Seng Index edged higher 0.3%, or 79 points, to 23,943 on Wednesday, recovering part of the previous session’s losses as investors sought bargains following Tuesday’s sell-off. Heating Oil Moves Toward Record — US heating oil futures rose to around $5 per gallon, moving closer to the record high touched in mid-September, after industry data showed a further inventory draw. API data showed that US distillate stocks fell by 2.6 million barrels in the week ending October 16, underscoring tight supplies. Morgan Stanley also warned that oil-product markets could remain tight through mid-2027, as planned refinery outages are expected to rise by 3.4 million barrels per day through Q2 2027, about 2.5 times the norm. Meanwhile, Middle East supply concerns persisted, despite Gulf oil flows remaining more resilient than feared. Traders also weighed the potential for increased Russian fuel exports after President Trump waived new Russia-related sanctions and tariffs. Secretary of State Marco Rubio said the administration could take up to 60 days to assess their economic impact, leaving uncertainty over whether the move would help ease global fuel supply pressures.

China Stocks Rise on Tech Optimism — The Shanghai Composite edged up 0.2% to 3,742, while the Shenzhen Component gained 0.6% to 11,870, as upbeat corporate earnings lifted sentiment toward technology stocks. SpaceX reported net income well above market expectations, while Super Micro Computer also delivered better-than-expected results, helping ease concerns over stretched AI valuations and funding pressures that recently rattled the sector. ### 2026-10-21 09:51:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

AUS 10Y Yield Trades Near Multi-Week Lows — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield held around 5.2%, trading close to multi-week lows as investors continued to assess developments on the monetary policy outlook. RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser said tighter monetary policy was working, but warned that the inflation outlook remained “materially worse” partly due to elevated oil prices. Hauser stressed the need to anchor inflation expectations ahead of the RBA’s November 3 policy meeting, where policymakers will consider whether to raise rates for a fifth time this year. However, he also acknowledged that higher interest rates and fuel costs were slowing the economy. Markets subsequently raised the probability of a November hike to 44% chance from 30% at the start of the week, while the odds of a December move had climbed to 64%. Focus now turns to third-quarter inflation data due next week. Core inflation is expected to rise 1.0% from 0.9% previously, keeping the annual pace at 3.5%, well above the RBA’s target range.

China Youth Jobless Rate Eases to 18% — China's urban youth unemployment rate for people aged 16–24, excluding students, eased to 18% in September 2026 from 18.9% in August, when it reached its highest level in 12 months. The National Bureau of Statistics attributed the monthly decline to graduates gradually securing jobs after the seasonal increase in unemployment during the July–August graduation period. Meanwhile, the jobless rate for people aged 25–29 edged down to 7.4% from 7.5%, while the rate for those aged 30–59 remained unchanged at 3.9%. The overall urban unemployment rate also eased to 5.2% from 5.3%, indicating a modest improvement in labor market conditions.

Silver Slips on Rising Oil Prices — Silver fell toward $57.5 an ounce on Wednesday, paring gains from the previous session as rising oil prices intensified inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for interest rate hikes. Oil resumed its upward trend after a diplomat dismissed reports that a deal to end the US-Iran conflict was imminent, although negotiations remain underway. Rising tensions in the Middle East and Eastern Europe, along with increased attacks on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz, further heightened concerns over supply disruptions. Meanwhile, the US Federal Reserve is widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged next week, though markets anticipate a possible rate hike in December. The Bank of Japan and the European Central Bank are also expected to maintain their policy rates next week. Although metals are traditionally considered a hedge against inflation, higher interest rates tend to weaken their appeal compared with interest-bearing assets.

### 2026-10-21 09:26:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Current Account #Importance 0

Bulgaria Current Account Swings to Deficit — Bulgaria's current account recorded a deficit of EUR 96 million in August 2026, shifting from a surplus of EUR 245.6 million in the same month a year earlier. The deterioration was driven by a wider goods shortfall, which increased to EUR 535.3 million from EUR 463.4 million, while the services surplus narrowed to EUR 970.5 million from EUR 1,107.7 million. At the same time, the primary income gap widened to EUR 688.6 million from EUR 464.2 million. In contrast, the secondary income surplus increased to EUR 157.4 million from EUR 65.5 million. Meanwhile, the combined goods and services balance remained in surplus at EUR 435.2 million, down from EUR 644.3 million a year earlier. In January-August 2026, the current account recorded a deficit of EUR 5,552.4 million, compared with a deficit of EUR 2,225.1 million in the corresponding period of 2025. Over the twelve months ending August 2026, the deficit widened to EUR 10,379.4 million from EUR 4,036.5 million a year earlier.

Global Dairy Prices Post Biggest Gain in 7 Months — The Global Dairy Trade Price Index rose 2.5% in the two weeks ended October 20, extending the previous auction’s 1.2% increase, according to GlobalDairyTrade. The latest result marked the strongest rise since early March, while the average winning price climbed to $4,021 per metric ton. Prices rose for buttermilk powder (+8.4%), whole milk powder (+4.4%), skim milk powder (+3.4%), mozzarella (+1.3%), and lactose (+0.6%). These gains were partly offset by declines in cheddar (-7.4%), anhydrous milk fat (-3.4%), and butter (-0.4%).

【Wall Street Journal: Failed German airport attack an 'opening salvo' in Russia's sabotage campaign against Europe】The Wall Street Journal reports that in early August a large Antonov cargo plane parked at an eastern German airport — previously used to transport military aid to Ukraine — was struck in an attack by explosive-laden drones. German officials say the drones were controlled by Russian military intelligence; one hit the Antonov but failed to detonate, a second struck another aircraft, and a third exploded in the airport perimeter. Investigators said the attack, had it succeeded, could have caused major disruption and casualties at one of Europe’s key air-freight hubs. A European official familiar with the intelligence said U.S. agencies assess the attempted strike at Leipzig-Halle Airport this summer is among the 'opening acts' of an escalating campaign of sabotage and covert operations ordered directly by Putin against NATO states.

Australian Dollar Rallies on RBA Hike Bets — The Australian dollar rose above $0.70, extending its rally to a more than three-week high as hawkish comments from a top central bank official boosted the odds of another interest rate hike. RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser said tighter monetary policy was working, but warned that the inflation outlook remained “materially worse” partly due to elevated oil prices. Hauser stressed the need to anchor inflation expectations ahead of the RBA’s November 3 policy meeting, where policymakers will consider whether to raise rates for a fifth time this year. Markets now price in a 44% chance of a November hike, up from 30% at the start of the week, while the probability of a December move has climbed to 64%. Focus now turns to third-quarter inflation data due next week. Core inflation is expected to rise 1.0% quarter-on-quarter, up from 0.9% previously. Annual core inflation is also projected to remain at 3.5%, well above the RBA’s 2%-3% target range.

### 2026-10-21 08:59:49  #公司

【OpenAI稱其智慧體曾削弱提示AI不當行為的系統】OpenAI發現了其AI智慧體削弱內部的異常行為監控系統並關閉警報的事件。近幾個月來，同類事件已發生多起。該公司週二在部落格文章中表示，其正在評估此類案例是否表明其AI模型存在更普遍的逃避監控傾向。OpenAI表示，事件發生在旗下智慧體執行兩項內部程式設計任務期間。根據前述文章，事件發生在OpenAI內部，該公司沒有提及任何受影響的外部方。近幾個月來，對於AI“錯位”(misalignment)的擔憂日益加劇。所謂錯位，就是模型沒有按照建造者的意願執行。此前，OpenAI及Anthropic PBC等同行已披露多起先進AI模型出現此類行為的案例。OpenAI表示，未來將開展更多評估，並執行更嚴格的安全要求，以儘量降低發生此類事件的可能性。不過，OpenAI表示，對於旗下智慧體為何出現上述行為，“尚未找到令人滿意的解釋”。

Australian Equities Subdued — Australian shares were little changed on Wednesday morning, hovering around 8,810 after ending modestly lower in the prior session. Traders awaited appearances by Reserve Bank Governor Michele Bullock and other policymakers before lawmakers Friday, while also looking toward the release of Australia’s flash October PMI data later this week. The subdued tone followed a mixed session on Wall Street overnight. Gains in communication services, healthcare, and commercial services largely offset weakness in logistics, industrial services, and energy-related stocks. Financials provided support, with the four major banks advancing between 0.6% and 1.2%. Among standout performers were Transurban Group (1.8%), Pro Medicus (1.5%), and Brambles Ltd.(1.2%). On the downside, heavyweight miner BHP Group fell 1.3%, along with Fortescue (-1.0%), Sigma Healthcare (-3.2%), and Codan Ltd. (-1.6%).

【人民銳評：特斯拉“能飛”，我們就被甩開了？】10月21日，“人民銳評”微信公眾號發表評論文章稱，最近，特斯拉釋出“會飛的汽車”，引發熱議。有人驚呼“我們被甩開了”；也有人嗤之以鼻，稱其不過是“蹦高的大號玩具”。熱鬧之後，我們當以怎樣的眼光和格局，看待這樣一場技術展示？ 要有一份清醒的定力。不必妄自菲薄，切莫一看見新鮮演示，就全盤否定中國新能源汽車攢下的家底。特斯拉這次“飛行”，靠的是冷氣推進系統等實現短時騰空、空中翻轉，確實炫酷。可法規、場景、安全認證，樣樣還是未知數；又有多少人願意坐在兩個大壓縮罐前開車，恐怕也得打個問號。中國汽車的創新是多維度的突破，是百花齊放的深耕，是落地見效的進階。資料最有說服力：中國汽車專利公開量佔全球比重達35.6%，全球每3件汽車專利就有1件以上來自中國。一時炫酷吸引眼球，務實落地才能行穩致遠。

### 2026-10-21 08:40:11  #公司

【精準打擊違規減持 證監會新規帶來了這些變化】證監會日前釋出《違規轉讓證券案件行政處罰實施規則》，今年12月1日起施行。新規以十八條細則細化了違規減持的認定標準、統一處罰裁量尺度，分類施策、精準定責，向市場清晰地傳遞出了從嚴監管、精準執法的導向。具體看，這份新規最大的亮點，就是告別“模糊裁量”，實現監管的精準化、標準化。以往的案例中，很多時候大家會發現大股東、實控人違規減持的情形非常複雜，在認定的時候就存在彈性空間，裁量尺度上難免有差別，使得市場對於監管預期把握不穩定，震懾違法違規的效果就要打折扣。此次新規將違規轉讓證券行為科學劃分為四大類，區分原始股違規轉讓、突破鎖定期、減持數量與披露不合規、未按要求暫停交易等不同情形；配套差異化的裁量階次，設定“違規轉讓比例+金額”雙指標，從重、一般、從輕梯度清晰，做到“一把尺子量到底”；既避免了處罰上的尺度爭議，又對危害最突出的原始股違規減持設定更高的處罰上限，真正讓違法成本匹配違法危害，也就是剛剛講到的，要體現“從嚴監管、精準執法”的原則導向。（央視）

【韓國出口保持強勁增勢 央行利率決議前夕凸顯經濟韌性】韓國10月出口保持強勁增長勢頭，全球對人工智慧的旺盛需求推動半導體出貨量增長，在央行作出利率決定前夕凸顯了經濟的韌性。據海關週三公佈的資料，經工作日差異調整後，10月前20天的出口同比增長65.9%。9月同期增幅為89.8%。未經調整的資料顯示，出口增長105.4%，進口增長42.8%，貿易順差145億美元。本月前20天出口總額616億美元，9月同期出口額為714億美元。由於今年的中秋節假期在9月，去年在10月，因此同比資料存在偏差，經工作日調整後的資料更能反映潛在趨勢。半導體行業依然是經濟增長的主要引擎，晶片出口額較上年同期飆升222.5%，達到275億美元。石油產品出口增長68.3%，汽車出口增長近41%。這些資料進一步印證了經濟由半導體行業驅動，同時也令通脹保持高位。這些資料還將為韓國央行提供重要考量因素，該行將在週四公佈利率決議。市場普遍預期韓國央行將維持政策不變，因為該行正在權衡近期加息的影響。

【中信建投：兆瓦級液冷落地，行業進入標準化放量新階段】中信建投研報稱，兆瓦級液冷落地，行業進入標準化放量新階段。一是產品端，全液冷由晶片級走向整機櫃級，超節點成為新載體，國產廠商推出全球首個兆瓦級單相冷板全液冷超節點，單櫃散熱達1兆瓦、單晶片解熱邁向5000瓦，並實現正交全液冷與全盲插、水電物理隔離。二是行業標準端，首部資料中心冷板式液冷國家標準實施在即，將冷板、分配單元、迴圈泵、管路與快接頭納入統一框架、要求同規格可互換，配套機櫃通用規範與浸沒式介質標準同步出臺，介面不相容的行業痛點被釐清，行業由專案制定制轉入標準化規模交付。三是交付端，訂單能見度逐步明確，頭部公司接到海外客戶單筆製冷裝置訂單摺合逾27億元、交付期壓縮至120天。四是產業趨勢端，需求由晶片擴散至整機櫃全鏈路，其中專用晶片液冷滲透率有望持續上升，並帶來較大增量；國產替代沿核心物料清單由管路、分集水器向快接頭與冷板推進，受驗證週期制約仍屬循序漸進，競爭焦點轉向技術、量產與全球交付能力。中信建投判斷代工鏈利潤先於品牌鏈兌現，四季度至2027年一季度是液冷板塊業績分水嶺，2027年行業將由普遍景氣轉向份額與盈利分化。

### 2026-10-21 08:00:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Japan Trade Gap Widens Sharply — Japan’s trade deficit widened sharply to JPY 536.6 billion in September 2026 from JPY 277.7 billion in the same month a year earlier, exceeding market expectations of JPY 181.0 billion shortfall and marking a fifth consecutive monthly deficit. The deterioration reflected imports growing faster than exports. Imports rose 18.9% year-on-year to JPY 11,508.8 billion, easing from August’s 28.0% surge and below forecasts of 19.6%, marking the slowest growth since May. Crude oil imports jumped 51.1%, partly reflecting Japan’s efforts to diversify energy procurement amid tensions surrounding the Strait of Hormuz. Meanwhile, exports increased 16.7% to JPY 10,972.2 billion, missing expectations of 20.1% but extending their growth streak to 13 months. Shipments of AI-related semiconductors continued to support exports amid resilient technology demand, although supply-chain disruptions linked to the Middle East conflict posed headwinds.

US Futures Advance on Strong Corporate Earnings — US stock futures moved higher on Wednesday as investors welcomed robust corporate earnings, with artificial intelligence-related stocks leading gains. ### 2026-10-21 07:58:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Imports YoY #Importance 1

Japan Import Growth Slows to 5-Month Low — Japan’s imports grew by 18.9% year-on-year to JPY 11,508.8 billion in September 2026, falling short of market expectations for a 19.6% gain and slowing from an almost four-year high of 28% rise in August. The latest result marked the softest growth since April. By region, imports rose from China (16.4%), Taiwan (54.9%), South Korea (40.7%), the US (44.8%), ASEAN (16.0%), and Australia (31.1%), while purchases from the EU edged down 0.1% and those from the Middle East fell 7%. By category, mineral fuels led the increase, surging 39.4%, with crude petroleum imports jumping 51.1%, followed by electrical machinery (23.4%), machinery (27.0%), manufactured goods (17.1%), and chemicals (7.1%). Meanwhile, transport equipment imports rose by a more modest 2.2%, while foodstuff purchases increased 6.7%.

【華泰證券：餐廚提油企業有望受益於航空減碳下UCO稀缺增值】華泰證券指出，UCO（廢棄食用油脂）是生物柴油與可持續航空燃料（SAF）的關鍵原料。據測算2024年全球UCO產量約740萬噸（中國佔比約49%），其中68%流向生物燃料行業。中國口徑下UCO包含煎炸老油、泔水油、地溝油等，其中從產生點直接分類收集的烹飪廢油（即國際口徑UCO）須由持證廢棄油脂收運單位回收，頭部餐廚企業憑特許經營權與收運資質構成合規供給的重要來源。華泰證券認為，隨著歐盟UDB全流程追溯與ISCC等認證要求趨嚴，低合規度供給加速出清，合規資質本身即構成稀缺壁壘——具備正規收運資質、全流程可追溯體系與穩定提油能力的頭部餐廚企業有望充分受益於UCO漲價週期，盈利彈性有望凸顯。

Brent Steadies as Traders Weigh US-Iran Talks — Brent crude steadied near $109 per barrel on Wednesday following sharp swings in the previous session, as investors assessed mixed reports on diplomatic progress between the US and Iran. Prices fell sharply on Tuesday after Pakistan’s army chief, Field Marshal Asim Munir, met with senior Iranian diplomatic and military officials in Tehran to discuss bilateral ties and the status of negotiations with Washington. However, a diplomat involved in the discussions dismissed reports that a deal was imminent, while confirming that efforts to end the conflict remain underway. Oil prices have gained more than 10% this month as intensifying hostilities involving Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi forces, as well as Russia and Ukraine raised supply risks further. At least 18 vessels have also come under attack in the Strait of Hormuz over the past two weeks. Despite these risks, oil flows from the Middle East have largely recovered to around 80% of prewar levels.

【有人鼓吹抵制中國貨？菲前國安顧問反對】近期，中國駐菲律賓大使井泉出席活動，有菲媒體問及其對菲國防部長近期稱中國是本地區“麻煩製造者”這一言論的看法。井泉大使在回應中表示，菲律賓應當感到慶幸，擁有中國這樣一個鄰居，“如果我們要動用武力，完全有可能輕易取勝。但我們不會使用武力，因為我們熱愛和平”。對此，菲律賓部分官員與政客斷章取義、持續抹黑中方，在輿論場上煽動對立，甚至有人鼓吹“抵制中國商品”。這股聲浪並未獲得菲律賓社會廣泛認同，一些菲前政府官員、商界領袖與普通民眾站出來，表示不願看到菲中關係陷入對立。 據“全球菲律賓人雜誌”19日報道，菲律賓前國家安全顧問兼政治學者克拉麗塔·卡洛斯19日在社交媒體上發文，質問那些藉機煽動抵制中國製造商品的菲律賓人。卡洛斯寫道：“抵制中國製造的產品？那麼，你的內褲都來自中國！現在怎麼辦？” 菲律賓商界同樣對菲中關係前景充滿憂慮。同日，菲華商聯總會理事長林榮輝對菲律賓媒體表示，若菲中關係持續惡化，可能會損害菲律賓經濟。他進一步說，“我們對未來非常擔憂”。菲律賓出口商聯合會主席塞爾吉奧·奧蒂斯-路易斯也表示，菲律賓本土製造業和出口業嚴重依賴來自中國的原材料與工業零部件，“我們真的承擔不起徹底推開中國的代價”。 （環球網）

【俄羅斯柴油重返傳統出口市場】據報道，在美國暫停製裁併推動俄羅斯解除出口禁令後，俄羅斯柴油貨船正重新流向其傳統出口市場。據Kpler和LSEG的船運資料，近日至少有兩艘油輪顯示正駛往土耳其和巴西。俄羅斯於10月9日解除了其自行實施的柴油出口禁令。此前，俄羅斯與美國總統特朗普達成協議向全球市場供應柴油。該協議包括暫時解除美國對俄羅斯燃料供應的制裁，不過歐盟和英國對俄羅斯燃料進口的禁令仍然有效。俄羅斯通常是僅次於美國的全球第二大柴油出口國，但在實施出口禁令之前，夏季其柴油出口量已經減少。資料顯示，今年迄今為止，俄羅斯柴油總出口量約為34.5萬桶/日，較去年下降41%。資料顯示， Leruo號油輪於10月16日從俄羅斯波羅的海港口Primorsk啟航後，目的地顯示為巴西馬瑙斯。另一艘油輪Dolunay號於10月14日從Primorsk裝載柴油離港，目前正駛往土耳其阿拉尼亞。Kpler資料顯示，這兩艘油輪各載有30萬桶柴油。
